Researcher Mark B disclosed Android CVE-2026-58882, dubbed 'SMS of Death.' A hidden WAP Push message can crash calling services with no tap; repeated delivery may cause reboot loops. This is denial of service, not device takeover.

An AOSP fix exists, but public bulletin timing and manufacturer rollout remain uncertain. Admins: inventory Android versions and verify device updates.

##
thecybersecguru@mastox.eu at 2026-10-31T16:49:09.000Z ##

🚨 ANDROID'S “SMS OF DEATH” 🚨

Technical breakdown 👇 https://thecybersecguru.com/exploits/cve-2026-58882-android-sms-of-death-explained/

An 8-BYTE SMS. Zero clicks. No notification. Your phone's calling service CRASHES.

CVE-2026-58882 exposes a dangerous flaw in Android's telephony framework that can:

📵 Kill active calls, including emergency calls
💀 Crash http://com.android.phone remotely
🔁 Trigger repeated crashes and boot loops
📱 Potentially leave devices unusable

The culprit? An unchecked integer conversion in Android's WAP Push parser.

Even worse, the message can bypass your normal SMS inbox entirely.

How does an 8-byte payload bring Android to its knees?

sayzard@mastodon.sayzard.org at 2026-11-02T19:43:42.000Z ##

A CVE-2026-98432 firewall rule for Ubuntu, with or without Docker

CVE-2026-98432는 Linux 커널의 IPv4 TCP 옵션 처리에서 발생하는 use-after-free 취약점으로, Ubuntu 26.04 커널에 대한 수정이 진행 중이다. 제안된 nftables 규칙은 호스트뿐 아니라 Docker 브리지 컨테이너로 전달되는 IP 옵션 포함 TCP 패킷도 차단한다. 이 조치는 노출을 줄일 뿐 취약점을 수정하지 않으며, 다른 네트워크 네임스페이스와 IPv6는 보호하지 않는다. 수정된 커널이 제공되면 설치 후 해당 커널로 부팅해야 한다.

https://photostructure.com/coding/cve-2026-98432-ubuntu/

DarkWebInformer@infosec.exchange at 2026-11-02T16:27:19.000Z ##

🚨 Critical Linux kernel flaw could allow remote root code execution; CVE-2026-98432

A use-after-free vulnerability in the Linux IPv4 TCP handshake path allows an unauthenticated attacker to trigger memory corruption by sending specially timed packets to a listening service.

Successful exploitation could crash the system or potentially execute arbitrary code as root.
⠀
• CVSS: 9.8
• Affected: Linux kernel 4.4 and later
• Attack vector: Network
• Privileges required: None
• User interaction: None
• Active exploitation: Not reported
⠀
Fixed kernel branches:

• 5.10.272
• 5.15.223
• 6.1.190
• 6.6.159
• 6.12.113
• 6.18.56
• 7.2.10
• 7.3
⠀
Administrators should install their distribution’s updated kernel and reboot to activate the fix.

Advisory: https://access.redhat.com/security/vulnerabilities/RHSB-2026-016

##
nixCraft@mastodon.social at 2026-11-02T15:28:37.000Z ##

WTF Ubuntu? https://chalmovsky.com/posts/wtf-ubuntu

In case you missed it: CVE-2026-98432 is a critical (CVSS 9.8) bug in the Linux kernel's TCP code. It's been there since 2016, so virtually every Linux server on the planet has it, and it can be hit remotely. Anthropic says it built a working exploit that gets root on a server. A total takeover.

Debian patched it on October 16. Ubuntu still doesn't have a patch more than two weeks later. Every supported release, LTS included, is listed as "Vulnerable".

##
technotenshi@infosec.exchange at 2026-11-01T06:45:08.000Z ##

Linux kernel CVE-2026-98432 is a use-after-free in the IPv4 TCP handshake that a remote, unauthenticated peer can reach. Upstream is fixed, but Ubuntu's tracker still lists its kernels as vulnerable and Red Hat lists RHEL 8, 9 and 10 as affected. Anthropic says Claude built a root exploit for servers with a multipath TCP listener, which Go 1.24+ enables by default. The Go team says the bug is not specific to MPTCP.

https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf

##
masek@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T21:33:12.000Z ##

@thilo CVE-2026-98432 requires close monitoring because it is a remotely trigger-able Linux kernel use-after-free vulnerability with demonstrated root-level exploitation.

Although kernel fixes exist upstream, they are not yet available or deployed across all distributions and supported kernel versions.

The demonstrated exploit currently requires MPTCP, but the underlying vulnerability affects ordinary TCP as well.

A use-after-free has already been demonstrated without MPTCP, although a complete exploit chain has not.

Disabling MPTCP is therefore not a reliable security boundary. It is a bandage.

A working exploit (which is not impossible) against ordinary TCP listeners could suddenly and dramatically expand the attack surface, potentially exposing virtually any unpatched Linux server running a reachable TCP service.

The key uncertainty is not whether ordinary TCP is vulnerable, but how far an attacker can exploit it. That makes continued attention essential.

##
cyberseckyle@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T15:01:05.000Z ##

Linux CVE-2026-98432 is a TCP use-after-free. Anthropic demonstrated remote root against an MPTCP listener in a lab, not known in-the-wild exploitation. The underlying flaw is not limited to MPTCP.

Go 1.24+ enables MPTCP on Linux listeners by default. Inventory exposed services, check distro advisories and deploy fixed kernels.

https://www.anthropic.com/research/discovering-vulnerabilities-with-claude

#Cybersecurity #Infosec #Linux #PatchManagement

Jpom 2.10.0 through 2.11.12 contains an authentication bypass vulnerability that allows unauthenticated attackers to gain super-administrator access by injecting query conditions into the JPOM-USER-TOKEN header. ##
DarkWebInformer@infosec.exchange at 2026-11-01T17:19:45.000Z ##

🚨 Critical LXD and MicroCloud authentication bypass rated CVSS 10.0

CVE-2026-112816 allows an unauthenticated attacker to impersonate a trusted TLS client using only its public certificate, potentially gaining full access to clusters, virtual machines and the host as root.
⠀
The attacker must be able to reach the HTTPS API and obtain the public certificate of an existing trusted client.
⠀
• CVSS: 10.0
• Affected: LXD 4.0.14, 5.0.10, 5.21.8 and 6.9
• Fixed: LXD 4.0.15, 5.0.11, 5.21.9 and 6.10.1
• MicroCloud fixes: 2.1.5 and 3.4.1
• Active exploitation: Not reported
• Public exploit: None reported
⠀
Administrators should update immediately and restrict access to the HTTPS API.

CVE-2026-119427
(4.2 MEDIUM)

EPSS: 0.22%

updated 2026-10-30T19:14:38.697000

1 posts

phpMyAdmin through 5.2.3 contains an sql injection vulnerability in Designer saveSetting() that allows authenticated users to inject SQL via unescaped json_encode() output. Attackers can send crafted index or value parameters with operation=save_setting_value to /database/designer to overwrite other users' designer settings or run controluser subqueries.

jbaggs@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T17:46:17.000Z ##

Welp...

phpMyAdmin -- Multiple vulnerabilities, no fix available

```Seven vulnerabilities in phpMyAdmin up to and including 5.2.3 were published on 2026-10-30. The most severe of them, an authentication bypass, is in the WebAuthn second factor: assertion signatures are never verified against the stored public keys, so an attacker who already has the MySQL credentials of a victim can skip the second factor.

The others are a server-side request forgery in setup/validate.php, which needs no authentication at all and can be used to probe internal hosts and ports; the removal of two- factor authentication without re-authentication; SQL injection in Central Columns and in the Designer, both running with controluser privileges; stored cross-site scripting in the Designer history; and a missing cross-site request forgery token.

There is no fixed release. The phpMyAdmin project has published neither a security announcement nor a new version, and only the request forgery has a fix in the development branch so far.```

CVE-2026-119427

CVE-2026-119563```

kmankarjr19 at 2026-11-02T20:30:02.213Z ##

3 threats SOC teams should know about this week 🛡️

My site Intel60 tracked 119 security stories this week. 34 were rated critical, and CISA added 10 bugs to its Known Exploited Vulnerabilities list. These are the three I’d put at the top of a SOC’s list:

1️⃣ Cisco firewall VPN zero-day (CVE-2026-76418)
Attackers are exploiting a flaw in the remote-access VPN of Cisco Secure Firewall ASA and FTD. It’s the second Cisco firewall zero-day since August.
🔎 SOC angle: patch now, then review VPN logs for unusual sessions. Edge devices are where attackers get in first.

2️⃣ Claude Code extension backdoored in Open VSX
The extension in the Open VSX registry was compromised to install a persistent SSH backdoor on developer machines. Cursor’s marketplace was also affected and temporarily shut down.
🔎 SOC angle: check developer endpoints for unexpected SSH keys and new authorized_keys entries. Developer tools are now part of the supply-chain attack surface.

3️⃣ Ivanti Connect Secure flaws added to KEV
CISA flagged a missing-authentication flaw (CVE-2026-111357) that can be chained with other bugs. The federal deadline is Nov 2, which tells you how urgent CISA considers it.
🔎 SOC angle: if you run Ivanti, patch, then hunt for signs of compromise. With Ivanti, patching alone hasn’t always removed attackers who were already in.

The pattern this week: the entry points are edge devices and developer tools. Watch what sits at the perimeter and what your developers install.

I track these with Intel60, a free tool I built that turns security news into 60-word, severity-rated briefs every 30 minutes: https://intel60.app

What was the biggest story on your radar this week? 👇

#cybersecurity #SOC #threatintel #infosec #blueteam #vulnerabilitymanagement

beyondmachines1@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T17:01:04.000Z ##

CISA Flags Two Exploited Ivanti Connect Secure Flaws That Give Attackers Root Access

CISA has added Ivanti Connect Secure vulnerabilities (CVE-2026-111357, CVE-2026-118527, and CVE-2026-104935) to its KEV catalog following reports of active exploitation. These flaws allow attackers to chain exploits to gain root access, upload malicious files, and compromise entire enterprise networks.

**If you run Ivanti Connect Secure VPN, upgrade right away to version 25.1.1.3 or 22.8R2. Attackers are chaining three flaws to take full control of the appliance, and older builds are still exposed even if you patched earlier in October. Treat any internet-facing appliance as possibly already compromised: after upgrading, change all passwords, API tokens and certificates stored on or reachable from the VPN, and check your Active Directory for suspicious accounts.**
#cybersecurity #infosec #attack #activeexploit
https://beyondmachines.net/event_details/cisa-flags-two-exploited-ivanti-connect-secure-flaws-that-give-attackers-root-access-k-d-m-s-l/gD2P6Ple2L

##
DarkWebInformer@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T16:22:14.000Z ##

🚨 Attackers chain three Ivanti Connect Secure flaws for unauthenticated root access

A previously unknown threat actor tracked as UTA0576 exploited three vulnerabilities to compromise fully patched Ivanti Connect Secure VPN appliances and execute commands as root without authentication.
⠀
• CVE-2026-104935: Authentication bypass and RCE - CVSS 8.6
• CVE-2026-111357: Privilege escalation - CVSS 8.8
• CVE-2026-118527: Predictable identifier allowing controlled file uploads - CVSS 4.3
• Affected: ICS 25.1.1.2 and earlier, and 22.8R2.5 and earlier
• Fixed: ICS 25.1.1.3 and 22.8R2.6
⠀
The actor extracted VPN and Active Directory credentials, searched for LAPS passwords and BitLocker recovery keys, conducted password spraying and deleted evidence from system logs.

CISA has added all three vulnerabilities to its Known Exploited Vulnerabilities catalog.

Source: https://www.volexity.com/blog/2026/10/13/a-series-of-unauthenticated-events-chained-exploitation-of-ivanti-connect-secure-zero-days/

CVE-2026-119563
(5.4 MEDIUM)

EPSS: 0.17%

updated 2026-10-30T12:31:31

1 posts

phpMyAdmin through 5.2.3 contains a stored cross-site scripting vulnerability that allows database users with CREATE privilege to inject script via unescaped table names in DesignerHistory.display(). Attackers can create a table whose name contains HTML markup that executes in another user's session when they add a condition on it in Designer query mode.

jbaggs@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T17:46:17.000Z ##

Welp...

phpMyAdmin -- Multiple vulnerabilities, no fix available

```Seven vulnerabilities in phpMyAdmin up to and including 5.2.3 were published on 2026-10-30. The most severe of them, an authentication bypass, is in the WebAuthn second factor: assertion signatures are never verified against the stored public keys, so an attacker who already has the MySQL credentials of a victim can skip the second factor.

The others are a server-side request forgery in setup/validate.php, which needs no authentication at all and can be used to probe internal hosts and ports; the removal of two- factor authentication without re-authentication; SQL injection in Central Columns and in the Designer, both running with controluser privileges; stored cross-site scripting in the Designer history; and a missing cross-site request forgery token.

There is no fixed release. The phpMyAdmin project has published neither a security announcement nor a new version, and only the request forgery has a fix in the development branch so far.```

CVE-2026-119427

CVE-2026-119563```

kmankarjr19 at 2026-11-02T20:30:02.213Z ##

3 threats SOC teams should know about this week 🛡️

My site Intel60 tracked 119 security stories this week. 34 were rated critical, and CISA added 10 bugs to its Known Exploited Vulnerabilities list. These are the three I’d put at the top of a SOC’s list:

1️⃣ Cisco firewall VPN zero-day (CVE-2026-76418)
Attackers are exploiting a flaw in the remote-access VPN of Cisco Secure Firewall ASA and FTD. It’s the second Cisco firewall zero-day since August.
🔎 SOC angle: patch now, then review VPN logs for unusual sessions. Edge devices are where attackers get in first.

2️⃣ Claude Code extension backdoored in Open VSX
The extension in the Open VSX registry was compromised to install a persistent SSH backdoor on developer machines. Cursor’s marketplace was also affected and temporarily shut down.
🔎 SOC angle: check developer endpoints for unexpected SSH keys and new authorized_keys entries. Developer tools are now part of the supply-chain attack surface.

3️⃣ Ivanti Connect Secure flaws added to KEV
CISA flagged a missing-authentication flaw (CVE-2026-111357) that can be chained with other bugs. The federal deadline is Nov 2, which tells you how urgent CISA considers it.
🔎 SOC angle: if you run Ivanti, patch, then hunt for signs of compromise. With Ivanti, patching alone hasn’t always removed attackers who were already in.

The pattern this week: the entry points are edge devices and developer tools. Watch what sits at the perimeter and what your developers install.

I track these with Intel60, a free tool I built that turns security news into 60-word, severity-rated briefs every 30 minutes: https://intel60.app

What was the biggest story on your radar this week? 👇

#cybersecurity #SOC #threatintel #infosec #blueteam #vulnerabilitymanagement

beyondmachines1@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T20:01:05.000Z ##

CISA Adds Fragnesia Linux Kernel Flaw to KEV Catalog Following PHP2Shell Chain Reports

CISA added the Fragnesia Linux kernel flaw (CVE-2026-46300) to its KEV catalog after reports of it being chained with PHP2Shell for remote root access. The vulnerability allows local users to overwrite in-memory system files to gain full administrative control.

**Make sure all industrial devices, especially Siemens SIMATIC S7-1500 CPUs, and your PAN-OS management interfaces are cut off from the internet and can only be reached from trusted networks. Then install the patched Linux kernel and reboot, and update PHP to 8.5.12 or 8.4.27. If you can't update, block the esp4 and esp6 kernel modules until you can.**
#cybersecurity #infosec #advisory #vulnerability

@varjolintu Since you didn't cite the CVE IDs I can't check what you're talking about, but CVE-2026-118673 looks like bs. AFAICT it's "user can exploit themselves".

This is exactly what I mean by slop CVEs. Bugs that are *of the form* of a vuln (memory corruption, UAF, etc.) but where there is not any (or at least not any significant) crossing of privilege domains that make it attack surface.

beyondmachines1@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T17:01:04.000Z ##

CISA Flags Two Exploited Ivanti Connect Secure Flaws That Give Attackers Root Access

CISA has added Ivanti Connect Secure vulnerabilities (CVE-2026-111357, CVE-2026-118527, and CVE-2026-104935) to its KEV catalog following reports of active exploitation. These flaws allow attackers to chain exploits to gain root access, upload malicious files, and compromise entire enterprise networks.

**If you run Ivanti Connect Secure VPN, upgrade right away to version 25.1.1.3 or 22.8R2. Attackers are chaining three flaws to take full control of the appliance, and older builds are still exposed even if you patched earlier in October. Treat any internet-facing appliance as possibly already compromised: after upgrading, change all passwords, API tokens and certificates stored on or reachable from the VPN, and check your Active Directory for suspicious accounts.**
#cybersecurity #infosec #attack #activeexploit
https://beyondmachines.net/event_details/cisa-flags-two-exploited-ivanti-connect-secure-flaws-that-give-attackers-root-access-k-d-m-s-l/gD2P6Ple2L

##
DarkWebInformer@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T16:22:14.000Z ##

🚨 Attackers chain three Ivanti Connect Secure flaws for unauthenticated root access

A previously unknown threat actor tracked as UTA0576 exploited three vulnerabilities to compromise fully patched Ivanti Connect Secure VPN appliances and execute commands as root without authentication.
⠀
• CVE-2026-104935: Authentication bypass and RCE - CVSS 8.6
• CVE-2026-111357: Privilege escalation - CVSS 8.8
• CVE-2026-118527: Predictable identifier allowing controlled file uploads - CVSS 4.3
• Affected: ICS 25.1.1.2 and earlier, and 22.8R2.5 and earlier
• Fixed: ICS 25.1.1.3 and 22.8R2.6
⠀
The actor extracted VPN and Active Directory credentials, searched for LAPS passwords and BitLocker recovery keys, conducted password spraying and deleted evidence from system logs.

CISA has added all three vulnerabilities to its Known Exploited Vulnerabilities catalog.

Source: https://www.volexity.com/blog/2026/10/13/a-series-of-unauthenticated-events-chained-exploitation-of-ivanti-connect-secure-zero-days/

karenit at 2026-11-02T21:10:15.699Z ##

Karen IT Security Response Center (@ksrc) Advisory

Advisory ID: KSRC-2026-11022

A #critical memory overflow #vulnerability has been identified in Citrix #NetScaler ADC and NetScaler Gateway. The vulnerability, tracked as CVE-2026-107406, has been assigned a CVSS v4.0 score of 9.5 (Critical).

An unauthenticated remote attacker can trigger a memory overflow condition that may lead to Remote Code Execution (RCE) or Denial of Service (DoS). The flaw is present when the appliance is configured as a SAML Service Provider (SP) or SAML Identity Provider (IdP).

DarkWebInformer@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T19:17:32.000Z ##

🚨 Chrome macOS GPU sandbox escape enables code execution outside the browser; CVE-2026-106378

Security researchers discovered that Chrome’s sandboxed GPU process could communicate with macOS WindowServer and inject keyboard input into the desktop.
⠀
The public proof of concept opens Spotlight, types “Calculator” and launches the application outside Chrome’s sandbox. The same technique could potentially open Terminal and execute commands.
⠀
The vulnerability is not a standalone one-click exploit. An attacker must first compromise a Chrome renderer or GPU process before using the flaw to escape the sandbox.
⠀
Key details:

• CVSS: 8.3 (CISA-ADP)
• Chromium severity: Medium
• Affected: Chrome for macOS before 155.0.8059.39
• Fixed: Chrome 155.0.8059.39 and later
• Public PoC: Available
• Active exploitation: Not reported

Technical analysis: https://pwn.ai/blog/chrome-escape

##
technotenshi@infosec.exchange at 2026-11-01T06:45:08.000Z ##

Linux kernel CVE-2026-98432 is a use-after-free in the IPv4 TCP handshake that a remote, unauthenticated peer can reach. Upstream is fixed, but Ubuntu's tracker still lists its kernels as vulnerable and Red Hat lists RHEL 8, 9 and 10 as affected. Anthropic says Claude built a root exploit for servers with a multipath TCP listener, which Go 1.24+ enables by default. The Go team says the bug is not specific to MPTCP.

https://atlasum.net/blog/cve-2026-98432-linux-ipv4-tcp-uaf

DarkWebInformer@infosec.exchange at 2026-11-02T16:07:35.000Z ##

🚨 Synology warns of ongoing C3RB3R ransomware attacks targeting outdated NAS devices

Attackers are exploiting a vulnerability patched in December 2024 to breach Synology devices running outdated DiskStation Manager versions, encrypt files and demand ransom payments.
⠀
Victims report files receiving the .L0CK3D extension alongside a read-me3.txt ransom note directing them to purchase a C3RB3R decryptor.
⠀
Synology has not identified the specific vulnerability being exploited. The linked advisory covers:

• CVE-2024-10441: Remote code execution
• CVE-2024-50629: Arbitrary file read
• CVE-2024-10445: Arbitrary file write
⠀
Users should immediately update DSM, enable automatic updates and remove the management interface from direct internet exposure.

Source: https://www.bleepingcomputer.com/news/security/synology-warns-customers-of-ongoing-c3rb3r-ransomware-attacks/

##
beyondmachines1@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T17:01:04.000Z ##

CISA Flags Two Exploited Ivanti Connect Secure Flaws That Give Attackers Root Access

CISA has added Ivanti Connect Secure vulnerabilities (CVE-2026-111357, CVE-2026-118527, and CVE-2026-104935) to its KEV catalog following reports of active exploitation. These flaws allow attackers to chain exploits to gain root access, upload malicious files, and compromise entire enterprise networks.

**If you run Ivanti Connect Secure VPN, upgrade right away to version 25.1.1.3 or 22.8R2. Attackers are chaining three flaws to take full control of the appliance, and older builds are still exposed even if you patched earlier in October. Treat any internet-facing appliance as possibly already compromised: after upgrading, change all passwords, API tokens and certificates stored on or reachable from the VPN, and check your Active Directory for suspicious accounts.**
#cybersecurity #infosec #attack #activeexploit
https://beyondmachines.net/event_details/cisa-flags-two-exploited-ivanti-connect-secure-flaws-that-give-attackers-root-access-k-d-m-s-l/gD2P6Ple2L

##
DarkWebInformer@infosec.exchange at 2026-10-31T16:22:14.000Z ##

🚨 Attackers chain three Ivanti Connect Secure flaws for unauthenticated root access

A previously unknown threat actor tracked as UTA0576 exploited three vulnerabilities to compromise fully patched Ivanti Connect Secure VPN appliances and execute commands as root without authentication.
⠀
• CVE-2026-104935: Authentication bypass and RCE - CVSS 8.6
• CVE-2026-111357: Privilege escalation - CVSS 8.8
• CVE-2026-118527: Predictable identifier allowing controlled file uploads - CVSS 4.3
• Affected: ICS 25.1.1.2 and earlier, and 22.8R2.5 and earlier
• Fixed: ICS 25.1.1.3 and 22.8R2.6
⠀
The actor extracted VPN and Active Directory credentials, searched for LAPS passwords and BitLocker recovery keys, conducted password spraying and deleted evidence from system logs.

CISA has added all three vulnerabilities to its Known Exploited Vulnerabilities catalog.

Source: https://www.volexity.com/blog/2026/10/13/a-series-of-unauthenticated-events-chained-exploitation-of-ivanti-connect-secure-zero-days/
